2025-06-14T23:52:49.2758063Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2758252Z <pr_title>
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2758892Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2759483Z </pr_title>
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2759617Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2759721Z <pr_description>
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2760709Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2761460Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2761551Z ## Problem
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2761840Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2762205Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2762452Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2762701Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2762945Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2763190Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2763403Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2763516Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2763904Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2764360Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2764435Z ## Solution
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2764535Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2764628Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2764923Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2765142Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2765929Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2767142Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2768088Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2768535Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2768685Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2769142Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2769427Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2769575Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2769914Z ```bash
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2770865Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2771725Z ```
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2771879Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2772021Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2772392Z ```bash
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2773393Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2774549Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2774940Z ```
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2775108Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2775355Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2775751Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2775910Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2776845Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2777729Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2777808Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2778055Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2778512Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2778992Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2779447Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2779978Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2780259Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2780816Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2781236Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2781317Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2781425Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2781428Z 
2025-06-14T23:52:49.2781517Z </pr_description>

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2738750Z     65.## Basic Usage Example
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2738962Z     66.

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2739502Z     68.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2739667Z     69.```python
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2739870Z     70.from prefect import flow, task
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2740104Z     71.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2740263Z     72.@task
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2740690Z     73.def prepare_plate():
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2740952Z     74.    print("Preparing plate...")
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2741240Z     75.    # Your OT-2 plate preparation code here
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2741518Z     76.    return "Plate ready"
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2741728Z     77.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2741893Z     78.@task  
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2742092Z     79.def run_protocol(plate_status):
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2742382Z     80.    print(f"Running protocol with {plate_status}")
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2742681Z     81.    # Your OT-2 protocol code here
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2742945Z     82.    return "Protocol completed"

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2743347Z     84.@flow

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2743756Z     86.    plate = prepare_plate()
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2744001Z     87.    result = run_protocol(plate)
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2744271Z     88.    print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2745867Z     96.## Troubleshooting
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2746088Z     97.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2746257Z     98.### Import Errors
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2746452Z     99.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2746626Z     100.If you get import errors:
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2746849Z     101.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2747097Z     102.1. **Check Python version**: Ensure you're using Python 3.10
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2747400Z     103.   ```python
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2747604Z     104.   python --version
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2747812Z     105.   ```
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2747984Z     106.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2748159Z     107.2. **Verify installation**: 
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2748388Z     108.   ```python
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2748643Z     109.   python -c "import pendulum; print('Pendulum OK')"
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2749109Z     110.   python -c "import prefect; print('Prefect OK')"
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2749391Z     111.   ```
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2749564Z     112.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2749753Z     113.3. **Check wheel compatibility**:
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2749995Z     114.   ```python
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2750303Z     115.   python -c "import platform; print('Architecture:', platform.machine())"
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2750826Z     116.   ```
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2751021Z     117.   Should show `armv7l` for OT-2
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2751261Z     118.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2751439Z     119.### Download Issues

2025-06-14T23:55:10.2758810Z     148.## System Requirements
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2759018Z     149.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2759227Z     150.- **Architecture**: ARMv7l (Raspberry Pi 3B+)
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2759502Z     151.- **Python**: 3.10+
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2759739Z     152.- **OS**: Opentrons custom firmware
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2760033Z     153.- **Network**: Internet access for initial download
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2760355Z     154.- **Storage**: ~20MB free space for all wheels
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2760773Z     155.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2760943Z     156.## Background
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2761128Z     157.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2761444Z     158.This solution addresses the complexity of installing Prefect on OT-2 systems where:
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2761826Z     159.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2762087Z     160.- Standard pip install fails due to compilation requirements
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2762480Z     161.- pendulum (Prefect dependency) requires Rust compilation 
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2762817Z     162.- ARMv7l wheels are not readily available
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2763115Z     163.- Limited system tools (no git, bash, venv)
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2763371Z     164.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2763731Z     165.The pre-built wheels in this repository eliminate these issues by providing compatible binaries.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2764152Z     166.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2764313Z     167.## Support
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2764490Z     168.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2764670Z     169.If you encounter issues:
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2764886Z     170.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2765093Z     171.1. Check that you're on an ARMv7l system (OT-2)
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2765379Z     172.2. Verify Python 3.10 is being used
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2765654Z     173.3. Ensure network connectivity to GitHub
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2765980Z     174.4. Try the automated installer first before manual methods
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2766388Z     175.
2025-06-14T23:55:10.2766664Z     176.For additional help, refer to the repository issues or documentation.

2025-06-14T23:58:09.3544672Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml`

2025-06-14T23:58:09.3545630Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3545746Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3546279Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547151Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547228Z ## Solution
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547338Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547428Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547733Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3547952Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3548274Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3548991Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3549545Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3549810Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3549902Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3550269Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3550614Z

2025-06-14T23:58:09.3551085Z ```bash
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3551792Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3552828Z ```
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3553139Z

2025-06-14T23:58:09.3553950Z ```bash

2025-06-14T23:58:09.3556013Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3556392Z ```
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3556550Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3556762Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3557098Z 
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3557242Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3558435Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-14T23:58:09.3559286Z
